- **geass**: Invoke the `geass-lock` skill — One-Shot Constraint Lock. Usage: `/geass &lt;constraint phrase&gt;`. Locks the constraint as absolutely inviolable for this session — visible in the brain block on every turn, not overridable by any advisor. Auto-expires at session end. Use `/geass off` to remove early. ([nexuslinkproductions/yuri-os](https://github.com/nexuslinkproductions/yuri-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/geass.md) · ★2)
- **gedaechtnis-cleanup**: Tidy the memory vault — duplicates out, over-long files folded into their archive, nothing deleted. Never asks; proposes until the vault has switched it on, then applies. ([justusotto/gedaechtnis](https://github.com/justusotto/gedaechtnis/blob/HEAD/commands/gedaechtnis-cleanup.md))
- **gedaechtnis-debrief**: End the session — the four-line debrief, then exactly what the Stop hook is about to commit. ([justusotto/gedaechtnis](https://github.com/justusotto/gedaechtnis/blob/HEAD/commands/gedaechtnis-debrief.md))
- **gedaechtnis-facets**: List a region's facets — the rule files beside its Boot file — and what loads each one. ([justusotto/gedaechtnis](https://github.com/justusotto/gedaechtnis/blob/HEAD/commands/gedaechtnis-facets.md))
- **gedaechtnis-graduate**: Give a region that has outgrown its boot budget a Boot file — measure it, draft it, show you the draft, and only then apply. Nothing is written without your word. ([justusotto/gedaechtnis](https://github.com/justusotto/gedaechtnis/blob/HEAD/commands/gedaechtnis-graduate.md))
- **gedaechtnis-recall**: Ask the vault what it already knows about something, and read the answer verbatim before deciding. ([justusotto/gedaechtnis](https://github.com/justusotto/gedaechtnis/blob/HEAD/commands/gedaechtnis-recall.md))
- **gedaechtnis-split**: When one topic has outgrown its region, give it its own — show what would move, take a name, and move it. Nothing moves without your word. ([justusotto/gedaechtnis](https://github.com/justusotto/gedaechtnis/blob/HEAD/commands/gedaechtnis-split.md))
- **gedaechtnis-status**: The facts about this session's memory — lane, vault, partition mode, dirty paths, boot cost, unanswered ledger rows. ([justusotto/gedaechtnis](https://github.com/justusotto/gedaechtnis/blob/HEAD/commands/gedaechtnis-status.md))
- **gedaechtnis-synthesis**: Ask the vault what it has learned and not yet acted on — a rule with no check, a lesson written down twice in two regions. Proposes; changes nothing. ([justusotto/gedaechtnis](https://github.com/justusotto/gedaechtnis/blob/HEAD/commands/gedaechtnis-synthesis.md))
